-- [MEEN HUB] Anti-Debug / Anti-Tamper check
do
  local __mh_t0 = os.clock()
  local __mh_sum = 0
  for __mh_i = 1, 50000 do __mh_sum = __mh_sum + (__mh_i % 7) end
  if (os.clock() - __mh_t0) > 0.15 then return end
  if debug and debug.getinfo then
    local __mh_info = debug.getinfo(1, "S")
    if __mh_info and __mh_info.what ~= "Lua" and __mh_info.what ~= "main" then return end
  end
end
